    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None

            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)

            # this is a leaf node:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs+1], True, None, None, None, None)

            new_node = Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        def dfs(n, r, c):
            if n == 1:
                return Node(grid[r][c] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)
            topLeft = dfs(n // 2, r, c)
            topRight = dfs(n // 2, r, c + n // 2)
            bottomLeft = dfs(n // 2, r + n // 2, c)
            bottomRight = dfs(n // 2, r + n // 2, c + n // 2)
            if topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val:
                return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)
            return Node(False, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

```
def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
    if level == 1:
        return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

    half = level // 2
    mid_r = rs + half
    mid_c = cs + half

    tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, half)
    tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, half)
    bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, half)
    br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, half)

    if (tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and
            tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val):
        return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
    return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        def dfs(n, r, c):
            if n == 1:
                return Node(grid[r][c] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)
            topLeft = dfs(n // 2, r, c)
            topRight = dfs(n // 2, r, c + n // 2)
            bottomLeft = dfs(n // 2, r + n // 2, c)
            bottomRight = dfs(n // 2, r + n // 2, c + n // 2)
            if topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val:
                return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)
            return Node(False, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        half = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + half
        mid_c = cs + half

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, half)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, half)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, half)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, half)

        if (tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and
                tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val):
            return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
        return Node(False, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

Let's decide final code exactly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        half = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + half
        mid_c = cs + half

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, half)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, half)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, half)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, half)

        if (tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and
                tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val):
            return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
        return Node(False, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        half = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + half
        mid_c = cs + half

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, half)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, half)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, half)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, half)

        if (tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and
                tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val):
            return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
        return Node(False, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        half = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + half
        mid_c = cs + half

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, half)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, half)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, half)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, half)

        if (tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and
                tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val):
            return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
        return Node(False, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

This is good. But note original `construct` uses `level` variable and passes `level` to buildTree. If `level` is 0, root None. Good.

Need maybe if `level` is 1, `buildTree` leaf. Good.

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        half = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + half
        mid_c = cs + half

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, half)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, half)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, half)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, half)

        if (tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and
                tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val):
            return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(False, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        half = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + half
        mid_c = cs + half

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, half)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, half)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, half)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, half)

        if (tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and
                tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val):
            return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(False, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
